# Graph Report - uca-t-0046  (2026-10-03)

## Corpus Check
- 1145 files · ~2,655,947 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 63 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 34, .mmd 8, .jsonl 5)

## Summary
- 23229 nodes · 49176 edges · 1042 communities (859 shown, 183 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 92% EXTRACTED · 8% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4054 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `d8b01075`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
